```json
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_5/projects/structfuse-context/ROADMAP.md\", \"content\": \"# StructFuse — Research Dashboard\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-13 18:11 UTC | 更新原因: D020 补充 Claim 2 判定矩阵 + n=3 功效限制 + plan_014 排期\\n\\n## 核心方向\\nInter-procedural data-flow analysis (CodeQL) 生成 structure-grounded hard negatives，用于对比式代码检索训练。详见 paper_framing.md。\\n\\n## 当前进展\\n- **阶段**: Research（post-MVP）+ 审稿进行中\\n- **M1 全量提取完成**: 123/131 repos, 44,173 instances, 15,153 有结构数据 (34.3%)\\n- **全量 Z%=78.0%**（条件）: Best Framing 确认（D015），artifact 已注册\\n- **Pilot 完成 (D16/D17)**: embedding > random 跨编码器一致; dataflow mixed\\n- **Full M2 训练中**: 18 runs 双卡并行 (D018)\\n- **Go/No-Go 阈值已锁定 (D019/D020)**: 结果出来前不修改\\n\\n## Go/No-Go 阈值 (D019 + D020 补充)\\n\\n**统计方法**: 3 seeds paired R@1 差, bootstrap 95% CI, paired t-test p<0.05。同时报告 best-by-val-loss 和 best-by-R@1 两种 checkpoint。\\n\\n**Claim 1 (dataflow vs random)**:\\n- **(A) Go**: dataflow > random CI>0 两个 encoder → 原 framing\\n- **(B) Conditional Go**: 至少一个 encoder CI>0 → 修正 framing + architecture discussion\\n- **(C) No-Go**: 两个 encoder CI 含 0 或负 → pivot 到 methodology analysis\\n\\n**Claim 2 (embedding vs random)**:\\n- **(i) Confirmed**: random > embedding CI>0 → embedding harms（与 MVP 一致）\\n- **(ii) Falsified**: embedding > random CI>0 → reframe（true embedding mining helps）\\n- **(iii) Inconclusive**: CI 含 0 → 报告 \\\"no significant difference\\\" + effect size\\n\\n**n=3 功效限制**（显式承认）: df=2, t 临界值 4.303, pilot 级别差异极难达显著。偏向 No-Go (Type II error 高)。若 (C) No-Go，论文 Discussion 必须讨论功效限制。\\n\\n## 近期任务\\n- [x] M1 + Z% + artifact + pilot + data build + 训练启动\\n- [ ] Qwen random_s42 首次 eval@500（~2-3h from ~17:30 UTC）\\n- [ ] 18 runs 全部完成 → D019 go/no-go 判定\\n- [ ] Full M2 完成后：plan_014 temperature ablation (~10h, 优先)\\n- [ ] Stage 3: Qwen 完成后立即启动 plan_003 SWE-bench eval（不等 StarCoder）\\n- [ ] 目录重命名 structfuse → structfuse-context\\n\\n## GPU 预算\\n- **bjb1-42106**: 2× RTX PRO 6000 96GB（专属）\\n  - cuda:0: m2_full_qwen 训练中（9 runs, ~3-7 天）\\n  - cuda:1: m2_full_starcoder 数据构建 → 训练（9 runs）\\n- **余量充足**: ~140 GPU-h needed / 576 GPU-h available。瓶颈是依赖链非总量\\n\\n## 关键风险\\n1. **n=3 统计功效**: pilot <2pp 差异在 df=2 下极难显著。Type II error 高，No-Go 不等于 \\\"无效应\\\"\\n2. **Claim 2 覆盖**: plan_005/014/015 未排期。Full M2 后优先 plan_014 (temperature ablation)\\n3. **Dataflow 过拟合**: 两编码器 train loss→0。Full M2 数据 3.4× 可能缓解\\n4. **Coverage 34.3%**: 实质全依赖 CodeQL（beniget 0.17%）\\n\\n## 关键决策日志\\n\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\n| D023 | 2026-05-13 | Kill M1 重提取：bjb1 CodeQL 查询 100% 失败，D022 前提不成立需诊断后重 | m1_reextract |\\n| D022 | 2026-05-13 | M1 28K instances 可恢复（运行时故障非数据问题）；当前 M2 继续，并行后台重跑 M | m1_extraction, plan_006_scalability |\\n| D021 | 2026-05-13 | Negative difficulty 分析揭示 encoder capacity 差异是 data | m2_embed_verify, plan_013_starcoder |\\n| D020 | 2026-05-13 | D019 补充：Claim 2 完整判定矩阵 + n=3 功效限制声明 | m2_full_qwen, m2_full_starcoder |\\n| D019 | 2026-05-13 | D018 补充：Full M2 go/no-go 量化阈值 + 统计检验方案 | m2_full_qwen, m2_full_starcoder |\\n| D018 | 2026-05-13 | 不做预判性 framing pivot，full M2 (18 runs) 是 go/no-go 唯 | m2_embed_verify, plan_013_starcoder |\\n| D017 | 2026-05-13 | Pilot 结果验证完成：embedding > random 跨编码器一致；dataflow 需按 | m2_embed_verify, plan_013_starcoder |\\n| D016 | 2026-05-13 | Reviewer Critical: m2_embed_verify 和 plan_013_star | m2_embed_verify, plan_013_starcoder |\\n| D015 | 2026-05-13 | 全量 Z%=78.0%（条件）确认 Best Framing；Coverage 34.3% 需论文讨 | m1_extraction, plan_006_scalability |\\n| D014 | 2026-05-13 | Kill m2_early_pilot (PID 795791) — 过拟合+卡死，bjb1 m2_ | m2_early_pilot, m2_embed_verify |\\n\\n\", \"lines\": 67}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me analyze what I'm seeing:

**GPU Status:**
- GPU 0: 100% util, 51621/97887 MiB, 69°C — PID 73558 (Qwen training)
- GPU 1: 100% util, 55187/97887 MiB, 71°C — PID 76627 (StarCoder training)

**GPU assignment check:**
- Registry says m2_full_qwen on cuda:0, m2_full_starcoder on cuda:1
- PID 73558 on GPU-41b18ac6... (need to map to index)


- PID 76627 on GPU-d831e380... (need to map to index)

Both GPUs are maxed out at 100% utilization with around 53-56% memory usage, which tracks with running 3B models at batch_size=8 with gradient checkpointing. But I'm noticing something alarming — there are duplicate Qwen processes running. Beyond the main PID 73558, I can see three additional Qwen training processes (PIDs 444209, 444210, 444211) that started at 07:16 and are executing the same command, each consuming roughly 3.6 GB of memory.

The strange part is these duplicates aren't showing up in the GPU processes list from nvidia-smi — only PIDs 73558 and 76627 are actually on GPU. So these three are stuck in CPU memory, possibly waiting to acquire GPU resources or having loaded the model but stalled out. If they somehow manage to grab GPU 0, they'll either crash with an out-of-memory error or tank the main training performance.

Meanwhile, M1 reextract v2 is running fine with its CodeQL processes and the extract script, which is CPU-only so no GPU conflicts there.

Now I'm checking the registry against actual progress — m2_full_qwen and m2_full_starcoder both show stale timestamps from 22:34 UTC, but it's been about 10 hours since then. They should've moved well past their recorded steps by now, so I need to pull the actual training logs to see where they really are. I also need to verify the GPU configuration in the project's execution settings.
```

_source line 61 · 2026-05-14T00:09:59.561Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Key findings:

**m2_full_qwen (cuda:0):**
- Currently at step 1550 in epoch 2/3 (wrapper log). The log shows eval@1500 with val_loss=0.5364, R@1=0.5228.
- Eval@1000 was better: val_loss=0.4651, R@1=0.5527. 
- Eval@1500 shows val_loss degradation (0.4651→0.5364) and R@1 drop (0.5527→0.5228). Overfitting signal!


- This mirrors what happened in the pilot run where performance peaked at step 500 before degrading.

**m2_full_starcoder (cuda:1):**
- At step 1000 in epoch 1/3 with eval@1000 showing val_loss=0.6134, R@1=0.4537, which is an improvement from the eval@500 checkpoint.
- Still on an upward trajectory.

**Duplicate Qwen processes (PIDs 444209/444210/444211):**
- All three are actively running and executing the same command as the original process.
- They're consuming about 3.6GB RSS each but aren't visible in nvidia-smi, suggesting they're CPU-bound.
- Started around an hour ago, which is much later than the main process, so they're likely spawned separately rather than being DataLoader workers—they have the full command line identical to the main invocation, not the typical worker process signature.
- The timing (exactly 3 processes starting simultaneously at 07:16) and memory footprint (similar to the forked main process) point toward Python multiprocessing workers, possibly spawned when epoch 2 began.
- Copy-on-write memory sharing means the RSS figures are misleading, and since they're CPU-bound rather than GPU-bound, this isn't a resource bottleneck.
- M1 reextract v2 is running normally across multiple repos with successful CodeQL queries and no recent errors.
- The registry shows about 15 of 82 groups completed as of an hour ago, and GPU assignments are properly aligned—Qwen on cuda:0 and StarCoder on cuda:1 match the registry entries, though I don't see an explicit gpu_ids constraint documented for this project.

Looking at the Qwen training metrics, there's a clear overfitting problem: validation loss jumped 15% and recall dropped 3 percentage points between eval@1000 and eval@1500, mirroring what happened in the pilot run. The dual checkpoint strategy will preserve the best model, but I'm wondering if running the full 3 epochs is inefficient when early stopping would have caught this sooner.

The registry notes indicate early stopping was deliberately skipped, and the dual checkpoint approach does protect against data loss, so this overfitting pattern is expected and handled. That said, the registry metrics are stale—last updated about 10 hours ago—and I should verify whether StarCoder has progressed beyond step 1000 to see if it's following a similar trajectory.
```
